While this is technically functional, `AutoGluon` is designed to be resource-intensive and often spawns multiple processes/threads. In a constrained environment, this approach is highly likely to hit resource limits (CPU/Memory) or cause race conditions when multiple folds are processed or when the `__del__` method attempts to clean up directories while processes are still active. Furthermore, the `__del__` method is unreliable for cleanup in Python. A more robust approach for this benchmark would be to use a lighter-weight model or a more controlled `AutoGluon` configuration that does not rely on `shutil.rmtree` in a destructor.
